## Action

Type

```ts
/** An entry in a menu, context menu or swipe action list: a command, a titled section of
 * entries, or a submenu. */
export type Action = ActionItem | Readonly<{
    section: readonly Action[];
    label?: string;
}> | Readonly<{
    id: string;
    label: string;
    symbol?: SymbolName;
    icon?: ReactNode;
    disabled?: boolean;
    submenu: readonly Action[];
}>;
// Resolved: Readonly<{ section: readonly Action[]; label?: string | undefined; }> | Readonly<{ id: string; label: string; symbol?: SymbolName | undefined; icon?: ReactNode; disabled?: boolean | undefined; submenu: readonly Action[]; }> | Readonly<{ id: string; label: string; accessibilityLabel?: string | undefined; symbol?: SymbolName | undefined; icon?: ReactNode; role?: "cancel" | "destructive" | undefined; disabled?: boolean | undefined; checked?: boolean | undefined; onPress?: (() => void) | (() => Promise<void>) | undefined; tint?: string | undefined; }>
```

## ActionItem

Type

```ts
/** A single command with an id, a label, an optional symbol and role, and an `onPress` handler.
 * Menus, swipe actions, alerts and confirmation dialogs all use it. */
export type ActionItem = Readonly<{
    id: string;
    label: string;
    accessibilityLabel?: string;
    symbol?: SymbolName;
    icon?: ReactNode;
    role?: 'destructive' | 'cancel';
    disabled?: boolean;
    /** Selection semantics for menu-style pickers. Defined false values reserve the checkmark column. */
    checked?: boolean;
    onPress?: (() => void) | (() => Promise<void>);
    tint?: string;
}>;
// Resolved: { readonly id: string; readonly label: string; readonly accessibilityLabel?: string | undefined; readonly symbol?: SymbolName | undefined; readonly icon?: ReactNode; readonly role?: "cancel" | "destructive" | undefined; readonly disabled?: boolean | undefined; readonly checked?: boolean | undefined; readonly onPress?: (() => void) | (() => Promise<void>) | undefined; readonly tint?: string | undefined; }
```

## AppDefinitionInput

Type

```ts
/** What an app declares about itself: identity, install metadata and launch appearance. */
export type AppDefinitionInput = {
    /** Built-in acknowledgements screen, available through navigation. Defaults to true. */
    acknowledgements?: boolean;
    /** A lowercase slug (`'lift-log'`) or a root-relative path. It keys storage and restoration, so
     * never change it after install. */
    id: string;
    /** The Home Screen name. */
    name: string;
    /** Defaults to `name`. */
    shortName?: string;
    /** Defaults to `name`. */
    description?: string;
    /** One square source image (`{ source: '/icon-1024.png' }`) from which the build derives every
     * size, or explicit manifest icons. */
    icons: readonly AppIcon[] | {
        source: string;
    };
    appearance?: {
        /** The app's accent colour, like an asset catalog `AccentColor`. */
        tint?: string;
        /** The light launch background. Defaults to the grouped system background. */
        background?: string;
        darkLaunch?: 'match-light' | {
            readonly background: string;
        };
    };
    /** Defaults to portrait on iPhone and every orientation on iPad. */
    orientations?: AppDeploymentInput['orientations'];
    capabilities?: AppDeploymentInput['capabilities'];
    /** Deployment overrides. Declare connectivity beside the app's other requirements. */
    advanced?: {
        /** Release keeps updates for the next launch; preview enables live updates in Safari tabs. */
        updateProfile?: 'release' | 'preview';
        installRoute?: AppDeploymentInput['installRoute'];
        platforms?: AppDeploymentInput['platforms'];
        viewportFit?: AppDeploymentInput['viewportFit'];
        launchScreen?: AppDeploymentInput['launchScreen'];
        mdm?: AppDeploymentInput['mdm'];
        appShell?: {
            additionalPrecache: readonly string[];
        };
        scope?: AppDeploymentInput['scope'];
        startRoute?: AppDeploymentInput['startRoute'];
        shortcuts?: AppDeploymentInput['shortcuts'];
        updateActivation?: AppDeploymentInput['updateActivation'];
    };
} & ({
    readonly connectivity?: 'local';
    readonly reachability?: never;
} | {
    readonly connectivity: 'server';
    readonly reachability: ServerReachabilityConfig;
});
// Resolved: { acknowledgements?: boolean | undefined; id: string; name: string; shortName?: string | undefined; description?: string | undefined; icons: readonly AppIcon[] | { source: string; }; appearance?: { tint?: string | undefined; background?: string | undefined; darkLaunch?: "match-light" | { readonly background: string; } | undefined; } | undefined; orientations?: { readonly fallback: OrientationFallback; readonly supported: readonly AppOrientation[]; readonly ipad?: { readonly supported: readonly AppOrientation[]; readonly fallback: OrientationFallback; } | undefined; } | undefined; capabilities?: readonly AppCapability[] | undefined; advanced?: { updateProfile?: "preview" | "release" | undefined; installRoute?: string | undefined; platforms?: "all" | "ios" | undefined; viewportFit?: ViewportFitPolicy | undefined; launchScreen?: LaunchScreenSlot | undefined; mdm?: { readonly origin: string; } | undefined; appShell?: { additionalPrecache: readonly string[]; } | undefined; scope?: string | undefined; startRoute?: string | undefined; shortcuts?: readonly { readonly name: string; readonly url: string; }[] | undefined; updateActivation?: UpdateActivation | undefined; } | undefined; } & ({ readonly connectivity?: "local" | undefined; readonly reachability?: undefined; } | { readonly connectivity: "server"; readonly reachability: ServerReachabilityConfig; })
```

```ts
    indexes?: I;
}) => CollectionDefinition<T, K, I>;
```

## Collection

Type

```ts
/** One collection of a database (`db.notes`). Once loaded, writes publish to memory at once, so
 * `useQuery` sees them immediately; the returned promise settles after the durable commit. */
export interface Collection<T extends object, I extends CollectionIndexes<T>> {
    get(key: string): Promise<T | undefined>;
    put(row: T): Promise<void>;
    delete(key: string): Promise<void>;
    query(configure?: (query: Query<T, I>) => Query<T, I>): Promise<T[]>;
    subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;
}
// Resolved: Collection<T, I>
```

## ControlStyleProvider

Value

```ts
/**
 * Sets control defaults for a subtree through `buttonStyle`, `toggleStyle`, `labelStyle`,
 * `controlSize`, `tint` and `disabled`. A control's own props win
 * over the provider, except that a disabled ancestor cannot be re-enabled by a child.
 *
 * @example
 * <ControlStyleProvider buttonStyle="borderedProminent" controlSize="large">
 *   <Button onPress={save}>Save</Button>
 * </ControlStyleProvider>
 */
```

```ts
/**
 * A row for picking a date, a time or both. Its grey value capsules open the system date or
 * time picker.
 *
 * Use a civil string selection (`'2026-10-14'`) for a day that must stay the same day in every
 * time zone, such as a due date; use a `Date` for a moment in time.
 *
 * @example
 * <DatePicker label="Due" selection={due} onSelectionChange={setDue} displayedComponents={['date']} />
 */
export declare function DatePicker<Selection extends Date | string = Date>({ label, selection, onSelectionChange, in: range, displayedComponents, datePickerStyle, disabled, 'aria-label': accessibleLabel, }: DatePickerProps<Selection>): import("react").JSX.Element;
// Resolved: <Selection extends Date | string = Date>({ label, selection, onSelectionChange, in: range, displayedComponents, datePickerStyle, disabled, 'aria-label': accessibleLabel, }: DatePickerProps<Selection>) => Element
```

## DatePickerProps

Type

```ts
/**
 * DatePicker's label, controlled selection, range and the components it edits.
 *
 * `selection` is a `Date`, or a civil string with no time zone: `'2026-10-14'` for a date,
 * `'09:41'` for a time, `'2026-10-14T09:41'` for both. The callback returns the same kind.
 */
export type DatePickerProps<Selection extends Date | string = Date> = {
    label: ReactNode;
    selection: Selection;
    onSelectionChange(selection: NoInfer<Selection>): void;
    in?: DatePickerRange<NoInfer<Selection>>;
    /** Defaults to `['date', 'hourAndMinute']`. */
    displayedComponents?: readonly DatePickerComponent[];
    datePickerStyle?: DatePickerStyle;
    disabled?: boolean;
    'aria-label'?: string;
};
// Resolved: { label: ReactNode; selection: Selection; onSelectionChange(selection: NoInfer<Selection>): void; in?: DatePickerRange<NoInfer<Selection>> | undefined; displayedComponents?: readonly DatePickerComponent[] | undefined; datePickerStyle?: DatePickerStyle | undefined; disabled?: boolean | undefined; 'aria-label'?: string | undefined; }
```

```ts
/**
```

## defineDatabase

Value

```ts
/**
 * Opens the app's local database, stored in IndexedDB. Each collection becomes a property of the
 * result. Bump `version` with a migration whenever the schema changes. Export and import are
 * built in, so offer them to the user.
 *
 * @example
 * export const db = defineDatabase({
 *   name: 'notes', version: 1,
 *   collections: { notes: collection<Note>()({ key: 'id' }) },
 * })
 */
export declare function defineDatabase<const S extends CollectionSchema>(config: {
    name: string;
    version: number;
    collections: S;
    migrations?: Partial<Record<number, DatabaseMigration<S>>>;
    /** Runs once on creation, after migrations, in the same atomic upgrade transaction. */
    seed?: DatabaseMigration<S>;
}): {
    [N in keyof S]: Collection<RecordOfCollection<S[N]>, IndexesOfCollection<S[N]>> & CollectionUpdateMethods<RecordOfCollection<S[N]>>;
} & {
    transaction<N extends keyof S & string, R>(names: readonly N[], action: (tx: Pick<TransactionCollections<S>, N>) => R | Promise<R>): Promise<R>;
    exportJSON(): Promise<Blob>;
    importJSON(input: Blob | string, mode: 'replace' | 'merge'): Promise<void>;
    onError(listener: (failure: WriteFailure) => void): () => void;
    close(): void;
};
// Resolved: <const S extends CollectionSchema>(config: { name: string; version: number; collections: S; migrations?: Partial<Record<number, DatabaseMigration<S>>> | undefined; seed?: DatabaseMigration<S> | undefined; }) => { [N in keyof S]: Collection<RecordOfCollection<S[N]>, IndexesOfCollection<S[N]>> & CollectionUpdateMethods<RecordOfCollection<S[N]>>; } & { transaction<N extends keyof S & string, R>(names: readonly N[], action: (tx: Pick<TransactionCollections<S>, N>) => R | Promise<R>): Promise<R>; exportJSON(): Promise<Blob>; importJSON(input: string | Blob, mode: "merge" | "replace"): Promise<void>; onError(listener: (failure: WriteFailure) => void): () => void; close(): void; }
```

## defineNavigation

Value

```ts
/**
 * Declares the app's primary tabs or its single stack before anything renders, so every stack
 * and tab id exists (and is typed) from the first frame. Pass the result to `runApp`; `TabView`,
 * `Tab` and `NavigationStack` then render it. Components may freely wrap those views.
 *
 * @example
 * const navigation = defineNavigation(routes, {
 *   tabs: [{ id: 'home', label: 'Home', symbol: 'house', root: 'home' }],
 * })
 */
export declare function defineNavigation<R extends Routes, const D extends NavigationDeclaration<R>>(routes: R, declaration: D & (D extends {
    tabs: readonly {
        id: infer I;
    }[];
} ? {
    defaultTab?: I;
} : unknown)): D;
// Resolved: <R extends Routes, const D extends NavigationDeclaration<R>>(routes: R, declaration: D & (D extends { tabs: readonly { id: infer I; }[]; } ? { defaultTab?: I | undefined; } : unknown)) => D
```

## defineRoutes

Value

```ts
/**
 * Declares every destination once. The same schema
 * types `useNavigation` and `NavigationLink`, validates restored paths and checks notification
 * deep links, so a route id or param is never a free-form string.
 *
 * @example
 * export const routes = defineRoutes({
 *   notes: route(NotesScreen, { title: 'Notes', titleDisplay: 'large' }),
 *   note: route(NoteScreen, { params: noteParams, title: 'Note' }),
 * })
 */
export declare function defineRoutes<const D extends Record<string, RouteDefinition>>(definitions: D): Routes<D>;
// Resolved: <const D extends Record<string, RouteDefinition>>(definitions: D) => Routes<D>
```

## ForEach

Value

```ts
/**
 * Makes one row per element of `data`, identified by its `id` (or an `id` function). In a
 * `List`, `onDelete` adds swipe-to-delete and the editing delete control; `onMove` adds the
 * reorder handle. Both receive offsets into `data`; apply them with `removing` and `moving`.
 *
 * Put `ForEach` directly in a `List` or `Section`: the list reads it to know each row's identity.
 *
 * @example
 * <ForEach data={notes} onDelete={offsets => setNotes(notes => removing(notes, offsets))}>
 *   {note => <NavigationLink to="note" params={{ id: note.id }}>{note.title}</NavigationLink>}
 * </ForEach>
 */
export declare function ForEach<T>(props: ForEachProps<T>): import("react").JSX.Element;
// Resolved: <T>(props: ForEachProps<T>) => Element
```

## ForEachProps

Type

```ts
/**
 * The collection, how to identify each element, the row each element becomes and its editing
 * actions. `id` may be omitted when the elements have an `id` field.
 */
export type ForEachProps<T> = {
    data: readonly T[];
    children: (item: T, offset: number) => ReactNode;
    /** `onDelete`: the offsets into `data` of the rows to delete. */
    onDelete?: (offsets: number[]) => void;
    /** `onMove`: the moved offsets and where they go, both counted in `data` before the move. */
    onMove?: (fromOffsets: number[], toOffset: number) => void;
} & (T extends {
    id: ListItemID;
} ? {
    id?: (item: T) => ListItemID;
} : {
    id: (item: T) => ListItemID;
});
// Resolved: { data: readonly T[]; children: (item: T, offset: number) => ReactNode; onDelete?: ((offsets: number[]) => void) | undefined; onMove?: ((fromOffsets: number[], toOffset: number) => void) | undefined; } & (T extends { id: ListItemID; } ? { id?: ((item: T) => ListItemID) | undefined; } : { id: (item: T) => ListItemID; })
```

## OpenURLResult

```ts
/** What happened to an open request: `handled` in the app, `dispatched` to the browser, or
 * `unavailable` with a reason. `dispatched` does not mean the page loaded. */
export type OpenURLResult = {
    status: 'handled';
    destination: 'app';
} | {
    status: 'dispatched';
    destination: 'web';
} | {
    status: 'unavailable';
    reason: 'invalid-url' | 'external-browser' | 'unregistered-app-url' | 'navigation-failed' | 'popup-blocked';
};
// Resolved: { status: "handled"; destination: "app"; } | { status: "dispatched"; destination: "web"; } | { status: "unavailable"; reason: "external-browser" | "invalid-url" | "navigation-failed" | "popup-blocked" | "unregistered-app-url"; }
```

```ts
/**
```

```ts
    sidebar?: string | ((params: RouteParameters<S>) => string);
};
```

```ts

 */

};
```

```ts
}
```

## useFocusChain

Value

```ts
/**
 * Moves focus through a form's fields in `order`. The return key goes to the next field and the
 * last one dismisses the keyboard. Disabled and unmounted fields are skipped. Spread
 * `field(key)` onto each field.

 * const focus = useFocusChain(['name', 'email'])
 * <TextField label="Name" {...focus.field('name')} value={name} onChange={setName} />
 */
export declare function useFocusChain<Key extends string>(order: readonly Key[]): {
    field(key: Key): {
        ref: (element: FocusChainField | null) => void;
        onSubmit: () => void;
    };
    focus(key: Key): void;
    dismiss(): void;
};
// Resolved: <Key extends string>(order: readonly Key[]) => { field(key: Key): { ref: (element: FocusChainField | null) => void; onSubmit: () => void; }; focus(key: Key): void; dismiss(): void; }
```

## WebAuthentication

Type

```ts
/** A sign-in flow made by `defineWebAuthentication`: prepare, start, complete and resume it, or
 * subscribe to its state. */
export interface WebAuthentication {
    /** Generate PKCE and durably register the transaction before enabling the sign-in button. */
    prepare(): Promise<WebAuthenticationRequest>;
    /** Validate and store the callback on the dedicated callback document. */
    complete(): Promise<WebAuthenticationCallbackResult>;
    /** Reconcile durable completion on launch, pageshow, focus and return from suspension. */
    resume(): Promise<WebAuthenticationState>;
    cancel(): void;
    getSnapshot(): WebAuthenticationState;
    subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;
    /** Attach bounded popup monitoring and lifecycle listeners. Returns their cleanup. */
    connect(): () => void;
}
// Resolved: WebAuthentication
```

```ts
} | {

    reason: string;
};

```

```ts
/** The OAuth provider, client, callback and app URLs for `defineWebAuthentication`, and the
 * `exchange` function that finishes sign-in on your server. */
export interface WebAuthenticationConfiguration {
    /** Stable name, unique within this app origin. */
    id: string;
    authorizationURL: string;
    clientID: string;
    /** Dedicated same-origin callback document outside appURL's scope. Never cache it. */
    callbackURL: string;
    /** The app's trailing-slash scope URL. */

    scopes?: readonly string[];
    /** Required for OpenID Connect; the server must verify issuer, audience, signature and nonce. */
    issuer?: string;
    timeoutMs?: number;
    /** Optional same-origin POST endpoint implementing the documented bridge protocol. */
    bridgeURL?: string;
    /** Establish your session on the server. Never return or message tokens to another window. */
    exchange: (request: WebAuthenticationExchange) => Promise<void>;
}
// Resolved: WebAuthenticationConfiguration
```

```ts

} | {
    status: 'failed' | 'unavailable';
    reason: string;
};

```

```ts
 * the frame's storage is partitioned from the app's. */
export declare function WebView({ url: input, title, controller, className, style, onStateChange, allowsJavaScript, allowsForms, }: WebViewProps): import("react").JSX.Element | null;
// Resolved: ({ url: input, title, controller, className, style, onStateChange, allowsJavaScript, allowsForms, }: WebViewProps) => Element | null
```

## withAnimation

Value

```ts
/**
 * Applies the state changes in `update`, then animates the
 * `MatchedGeometryEffect` views they move, insert or remove. Call it from an event handler.
 * Keyed List rows also move to their new positions. Other views take their new layout at once.
 * Returns the callback result and passes errors through. Only synchronous updates take part,
 * and nested calls join the outer one.
 * @example
 * <Button onPress={() => withAnimation(() => setShowQueue(!showQueue))}>Playing Next</Button>
 */
export declare function withAnimation<Result>(update: () => Result): Result;
export declare function withAnimation<Result>(animation: Animation, update: () => Result): Result;
// Resolved: { <Result>(update: () => Result): Result; <Result>(animation: Readonly<{ [brand]: true; }>, update: () => Result): Result; }
```

```ts
 * time zone, such as a due date; use a `Date` for a moment in time.

 * <DatePicker label="Due" selection={due} onSelectionChange={setDue} displayedComponents={['date']} />
 */
export declare function DatePicker<Selection extends Date | string = Date>({ label, selection, onSelectionChange, in: range, displayedComponents, datePickerStyle, disabled, 'aria-label': accessibleLabel, }: DatePickerProps<Selection>): import("react").JSX.Element;

}

    name: string;

    seed?: DatabaseMigration<S>;
}): {
    [N in keyof S]: Collection<RecordOfCollection<S[N]>, IndexesOfCollection<S[N]>> & CollectionUpdateMethods<RecordOfCollection<S[N]>>;

    exportJSON(): Promise<Blob>;

    onError(listener: (failure: WriteFailure) => void): () => void;
    close(): void;
};

/**

 *
 * Put `ForEach` directly in a `List` or `Section`: the list reads it to know each row's identity.

 * <ForEach data={notes} onDelete={offsets => setNotes(notes => removing(notes, offsets))}>

// list-form/offsets.d.ts :: moving
/**
 * Returns a new array: the elements at `fromOffsets` move,
 * in their original order, to just before the element that was at `toOffset`. Pass it exactly
 * what `ForEach`'s `onMove` reports.
 *
 * `toOffset` counts positions in the original array: moving the first of three
 * rows below the second reports `toOffset` 2, not 1. Use `items.length` to move to the end.
 *
 * @example
 * <ForEach
 *   data={todos}
 *   onMove={(from, to) => setTodos(todos => moving(todos, { fromOffsets: from, toOffset: to }))}
 * >
 */
export declare function moving<T>(items: readonly T[], { fromOffsets, toOffset }: {
    fromOffsets: readonly number[];
    toOffset: number;
}): T[];

// list-form/offsets.d.ts :: offsets
offsets: readonly number[]

// list-form/offsets.d.ts :: removing
/**
 * Returns a new array without the elements at `offsets`. Pass it exactly what `ForEach`'s
 * `onDelete` reports.
 *
 * @example
 * <ForEach data={todos} onDelete={offsets => setTodos(todos => removing(todos, offsets))}>
 */
export declare function removing<T>(items: readonly T[], offsets: readonly number[]): T[];

// media/types.d.ts :: MediaCommandResult
/** How a media command ended. `needs-user-activation` means WebKit wants a tap first. */
export type MediaCommandResult = Readonly<{
    status: 'completed' | 'cancelled' | 'needs-user-activation' | 'unavailable';
    operationId: number;
} | {
    status: 'failed';
    operationId: number;
    code: string;
    cause?: unknown;
}>;

// media/types.d.ts :: MediaController
/** The audio player's commands: queue editing, transport, seek, repeat and shuffle. Queue
 * commands resolve once the change is made; `play` and `seek` resolve once playback is seen to
 * respond. */
export interface MediaController {
    replaceQueue(entries: readonly MediaQueueEntry[], options?: MediaQueueOptions): Promise<MediaCommandResult>;
    play(): Promise<MediaCommandResult>;
    pause(): MediaCommandResult;
    stop(): MediaCommandResult;
    seek(seconds: number): Promise<MediaCommandResult>;
    next(): Promise<MediaCommandResult>;
    previous(): Promise<MediaCommandResult>;
    append(entries: readonly MediaQueueEntry[]): MediaCommandResult;
    insertNext(entries: readonly MediaQueueEntry[]): MediaCommandResult;
    remove(entryId: string): MediaCommandResult;
    move(entryId: string, beforeEntryId: string | null): MediaCommandResult;
    setRepeat(repeat: MediaRepeat): MediaCommandResult;
    setShuffle(shuffle: boolean): MediaCommandResult;
}

// media/types.d.ts :: mediaId
mediaId: string;

// media/types.d.ts :: MediaPlayer
/** `useMediaPlayer`'s result: the commands and the live state. */
export type MediaPlayer = Readonly<{
    controller: MediaController;
    state: MediaSnapshot;
}>;

// media/types.d.ts :: MediaQueueEntry
/** One item in the audio queue: its source URL, media id and display metadata. Each occurrence
 * has its own `entryId`, so a repeated song appears twice. */
export type MediaQueueEntry = Readonly<{
    entryId: string;
    mediaId: string;
    src: string;
    mimeType?: string;
    sourceRevision?: string | number;
    metadata?: Readonly<{
        title?: string;
        artist?: string;
        album?: string;
        artwork?: string;
    }>;
}>;

// media/types.d.ts :: MediaQueueOptions
/** Where a replaced queue starts, and whether it plays. */
export type MediaQueueOptions = Readonly<{
    currentEntryId?: string;
    position?: number;
    autoplay?: boolean;
}>;

// media/types.d.ts :: MediaRepeat
/** The queue's repeat mode: `off`, `one` or `all`. */
export type MediaRepeat = 'off' | 'one' | 'all';

// media/types.d.ts :: MediaSnapshot
/** Everything the player shows: the queue, the current entry, status, position and modes. */
export type MediaSnapshot = Readonly<{
    queue: readonly MediaQueueEntry[];
    playbackOrder: readonly string[];
    currentEntryId: string | null;
    status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'paused' | 'playing' | 'buffering' | 'ended' | 'interrupted' | 'error';
    position: number;
    duration: Readonly<{
        kind: 'unknown' | 'live';
    }> | Readonly<{
        kind: 'finite';
        seconds: number;
    }>;
    seekable: readonly Readonly<{
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }>[];
    repeat: MediaRepeat;
    shuffle: boolean;
    interruption: 'unknown-system' | null;
    error: Readonly<{
        code: string;
    }> | null;
}>;

/**

 */

/** A scope for matched-geometry ids. Two views match only if they share an id and a namespace. */

    [namespaceBrand]: true;
}>;

// motion/matchedGeometry.d.ts :: useNamespace
/** Returns a namespace that stays the same for the life of the component. Pass it to
 * `MatchedGeometryEffect` and `MatchedTransitionSource`. */
export declare function useNamespace(): NamespaceID;

// motion/matchedGeometry.d.ts :: withAnimation
/**
 * Applies the state changes in `update`, then animates the
 * `MatchedGeometryEffect` views they move, insert or remove. Call it from an event handler.
 * Keyed List rows also move to their new positions. Other views take their new layout at once.
 * Returns the callback result and passes errors through. Only synchronous updates take part,
 * and nested calls join the outer one.
 * @example
 * <Button onPress={() => withAnimation(() => setShowQueue(!showQueue))}>Playing Next</Button>
 */
export declare function withAnimation<Result>(update: () => Result): Result;

// motion/matchedGeometry.d.ts :: withAnimation
export declare function withAnimation<Result>(animation: Animation, update: () => Result): Result;

 * toolbar, `ForEach`'s `onDelete` and `onMove` add the editing controls.
 *
 * @example
 * <List id="notes">
 *   {notes.map(note => (

 *       {note.title}

 *   ))}
 * </List>

/**

 * @example
 * <Picker label="Sort" selection={sort} onSelectionChange={setSort}
 *   options={[{ id: 'date', label: 'Date' }, { id: 'title', label: 'Title' }]} />

 *   options={[{ id: 'home', label: 'Home', symbol: 'house', color: systemColor('red') }]} />

    restoration?: 'restore' | 'skip';
    /** The sidebar row this destination selects in a `NavigationSplitView` (default: its id). */

};

// safe-area/SafeAreaInset.d.ts :: SafeAreaInset
/**
 * Pins an accessory, such as a composer or a filter bar, to one edge of its content. The
 * accessory takes room from the content's safe area on its edge; a scroll view inside still
 * spans the whole frame and scrolls under it.
 *
 * At the bottom, content scrolls under the accessory and its last item stays clear of it. When
 * the accessory resizes, the scroll inset changes in the same frame (through
 * `--satin-inset-accessory-height`), so a composer that grows by a line keeps the newest message
 * in view.
 */
export declare function SafeAreaInset({ edge, content, spacing, children, ...props }: SafeAreaInsetProps): import("react").JSX.Element;

// search/searchable.d.ts :: Searchable
/**
 * A search field for a screen or list: its controlled text, prompt, placement, scopes and
 * suggestions. The bar owns the field: its capsule, magnifier, clear button, close button, scope
 * bar and collapse on scroll. In the bottom toolbar, the presented field rides above the
 * keyboard with a close button, and the bar and title give way. Place
 * `<DefaultToolbarItem kind="search" placement="bottomBar" />` to put it beside other bottom bar
 * items.
 */
export type Searchable = Readonly<{
    text: string;
    onTextChange(text: string): void;
    prompt?: string;
    /** Default `'automatic'`. On iPhone a field with `scopes` uses the drawer. */
    placement?: SearchFieldPlacement;
    scopes?: SearchScopes;
    suggestions?: readonly SearchSuggestion[];
    onSubmit?(): void;
} & ({
    isPresented?: never;
    onPresentedChange?(presented: boolean): void;
} | {
    isPresented: boolean;
    onPresentedChange(presented: boolean): void;
})>;

// search/searchable.d.ts :: SearchFieldPlacement
/** Where the search field goes. On iPhone, `'automatic'` uses the bottom toolbar outside a
 * `TabView` when the screen has no other bottom bar items or scopes; otherwise it uses a drawer
 * that scrolls away with the content. On iPad, automatic search belongs to the trailing
 * navigation toolbar, or below the sidebar toolbar in a split view. Explicit toolbar
 * placement stays trailing on iPad, including narrow windows. */
export type SearchFieldPlacement = 'automatic' | 'navigationBarDrawer' | 'toolbar';

// search/searchable.d.ts :: SearchScopes
/** A controlled scope bar, shown under the search field while search is presented. */
export type SearchScopes = Readonly<{
    selection: string;
    onSelectionChange(selection: string): void;
    options: readonly Readonly<{
        id: string;
        label: string;
    }>[];
}>;

// search/searchable.d.ts :: SearchSuggestion
/** A suggestion shown under the search field. Choosing one replaces the search text; it does not
 * submit. */
export type SearchSuggestion = Readonly<{
    id: string;
    text: string;
    label?: ReactNode;
}>;

// tasks/useTask.d.ts :: TaskAction
/** The work `useTask` runs: pass `signal` to fetches and publish state through `commit`. */
export type TaskAction = (signal: AbortSignal, commit: TaskCommit) => void | Promise<void>;

// tasks/useTask.d.ts :: TaskCommit
/** Publish synchronous state updates only while this invocation is current. */
export type TaskCommit = (update: () => void) => boolean;

// tasks/useTask.d.ts :: TaskOptions
/** Options for `useTask`. A change of `id` cancels the current run and starts a new one. */
export type TaskOptions = Readonly<{
    id?: unknown;
}>;

// tasks/useTask.d.ts :: TaskRunner
/** Cancels the current run and starts the task again; resolves when that run ends. */
export type TaskRunner = () => Promise<void>;

// tasks/useTask.d.ts :: update
update: () => void

// tasks/useTask.d.ts :: useTask
/**
 * Runs async work while its screen is showing. It cancels the run when the screen is covered,
 * its tab is hidden or the component unmounts, and starts again when it shows. Pass `signal` to
 * `fetch` and publish results through `commit`. An error the task throws reaches the nearest
 * error boundary. The returned runner cancels and replaces the current run.
 */
export declare function useTask(action: TaskAction, { id }?: TaskOptions): TaskRunner;

// text-input/focusChain.d.ts :: useFocusChain
/**
 * Moves focus through a form's fields in `order`. The return key goes to the next field and the
 * last one dismisses the keyboard. Disabled and unmounted fields are skipped. Spread
 * `field(key)` onto each field.
 *
 * @example
 * const focus = useFocusChain(['name', 'email'])
 * <TextField label="Name" {...focus.field('name')} value={name} onChange={setName} />
 */
export declare function useFocusChain<Key extends string>(order: readonly Key[]): {
    field(key: Key): {
        ref: (element: FocusChainField | null) => void;
        onSubmit: () => void;
    };
    focus(key: Key): void;
    dismiss(): void;
};

export type SubmitLabel = 'done' | 'go' | 'next' | 'search' | 'send';

/** What a field holds, setting its keyboard, autofill and autocorrection together. */
export type TextFieldKind = 'text' | 'name' | 'email' | 'url' | 'tel' | 'number' | 'decimal' | 'search' | 'username' | 'new-password' | 'current-password' | 'one-time-code';

// web/authentication.d.ts :: WebAuthentication
/** A sign-in flow made by `defineWebAuthentication`: prepare, start, complete and resume it, or
 * subscribe to its state. */
export interface WebAuthentication {
    /** Generate PKCE and durably register the transaction before enabling the sign-in button. */
    prepare(): Promise<WebAuthenticationRequest>;
    /** Validate and store the callback on the dedicated callback document. */
    complete(): Promise<WebAuthenticationCallbackResult>;
    /** Reconcile durable completion on launch, pageshow, focus and return from suspension. */
    resume(): Promise<WebAuthenticationState>;
    cancel(): void;
    getSnapshot(): WebAuthenticationState;
    subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;
    /** Attach bounded popup monitoring and lifecycle listeners. Returns their cleanup. */
    connect(): () => void;
}

// web/authentication.d.ts :: WebAuthenticationCallbackResult
/** The outcome of `complete` on the callback page: the URL to return to, or why it was rejected. */
export type WebAuthenticationCallbackResult = {
    status: 'completed';
    returnURL: string;
} | {
    status: 'rejected' | 'unavailable';
    reason: string;
};

// web/authentication.d.ts :: WebAuthenticationConfiguration
/** The OAuth provider, client, callback and app URLs for `defineWebAuthentication`, and the
 * `exchange` function that finishes sign-in on your server. */
export interface WebAuthenticationConfiguration {
    /** Stable name, unique within this app origin. */
    id: string;
    authorizationURL: string;
    clientID: string;
    /** Dedicated same-origin callback document outside appURL's scope. Never cache it. */
    callbackURL: string;
    /** The app's trailing-slash scope URL. */
    appURL: string;
    scopes?: readonly string[];
    /** Required for OpenID Connect; the server must verify issuer, audience, signature and nonce. */
    issuer?: string;
    timeoutMs?: number;
    /** Optional same-origin POST endpoint implementing the documented bridge protocol. */
    bridgeURL?: string;
    /** Establish your session on the server. Never return or message tokens to another window. */
    exchange: (request: WebAuthenticationExchange) => Promise<void>;
}

// web/authentication.d.ts :: WebAuthenticationExchange
/** What `exchange` receives: the authorization code, the PKCE verifier and the values your
 * server needs to redeem and check it. */
export interface WebAuthenticationExchange {
    code: string;
    codeVerifier: string;
    callbackURL: string;

/** A prepared sign-in. Call `start` from the user's press. */

/** Whether `start` opened the provider in a popup or a redirect, or why it could not. */

} | {

// web/authentication.d.ts :: WebAuthenticationState
/** Where a sign-in stands, from `idle` through `pending` and `completing` to a final status. */

    needsReturn: boolean;
} | {
    status: 'succeeded' | 'cancelled' | 'expired';
} | {
    status: 'failed' | 'unavailable';
    reason: string;
};

// web/WebView.d.ts :: WebFrameActionResult
/** Whether a `reload` or `stop` request was sent to the frame, or why not. */
export type WebFrameActionResult = {

// web/WebView.d.ts :: WebFrameController
/** Reads a `WebView`'s state and sends it `reload` and `stop`. Only same-origin frames expose
 * their current page or accept these commands. */

    readonly state: WebFrameState;

// web/WebView.d.ts :: WebFrameState
/** The embedded page's load state. A cross-origin page reports `unverified`, because its contents
 * cannot be read. */
export type WebFrameState = {
    status: 'idle' | 'loading';
} | {
    status: 'loaded';
    url: string;
    title: string;
} | {
    status: 'unverified';
    reason: 'cross-origin-or-denied';
} | {
    status: 'unavailable';
    reason: 'invalid-url' | 'load-error';
};

 * the frame's storage is partitioned from the app's. */

 * and forms are off by default. */
export interface WebViewProps {
    url: string;
    /** Accessible name for the embedded page. */
    title: string;
    controller?: WebFrameController;
    /** Explicitly allow scripts. Same-origin scripted content must be trusted. */
    allowsJavaScript?: boolean;
    allowsForms?: boolean;
    className?: string;
    style?: CSSProperties;
    onStateChange?: (state: WebFrameState) => void;
}
```
